// KayKit Asset Loader with Lazy Loading
(function() {
    const ASSET_BASE = '/assets';
    const models = new Map();
    let config = null;
    let configReady = false;

    async function loadJSON(path) {
        try {
            const response = await fetch(path);
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
            return await response.json();
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn(`KayKitLoader: failed to load ${path}`, error);
            return null;
        }
    }

    async function loadGLTF(path) {
        if (!THREE || !THREE.GLTFLoader) {
            console.warn('KayKitLoader: GLTFLoader not available');
            return null;
        }
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const loader = new THREE.GLTFLoader();
            loader.load(
                path,
                gltf => resolve(gltf.scene),
                undefined,
                error => reject(error)
            );
        });
    }

    async function init() {
        config = await loadJSON(`${ASSET_BASE}/room_config_detailed.json`);
        if (!config || !config.rooms) {
            console.warn('KayKitLoader: no valid config found');
            config = { rooms: {}, defaults: { span: 24, wallHeight: 6 } };
        }
        configReady = true;
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kaykit:configReady'));

        // Preload only Wohnzimmer assets
        await preloadRoomAssets('Wohnzimmer');
    }

    async function preloadRoomAssets(roomName) {
        const roomConfig = config && config.rooms && config.rooms[roomName];
        if (!roomConfig) return;

        const assets = new Set();
        if (roomConfig.floor && roomConfig.floor.model) assets.add(roomConfig.floor.model);
        if (roomConfig.walls && roomConfig.walls.model) assets.add(roomConfig.walls.model);
        if (Array.isArray(roomConfig.props)) {
            roomConfig.props.forEach(prop => {
                if (prop && prop.model) assets.add(prop.model);
            });
        }

        for (const modelName of assets) {
            if (!models.has(modelName)) {
                await loadModel(modelName);
            }
        }
    }

    async function loadRoomOnDemand(roomName) {
        const roomConfig = config && config.rooms && config.rooms[roomName];
        if (!roomConfig) return;

        const assets = new Set();
        if (roomConfig.floor && roomConfig.floor.model) assets.add(roomConfig.floor.model);
        if (roomConfig.walls && roomConfig.walls.model) assets.add(roomConfig.walls.model);
        if (Array.isArray(roomConfig.props)) {
            roomConfig.props.forEach(prop => {
                if (prop && prop.model) assets.add(prop.model);
            });
        }

        // Load only missing assets
        for (const modelName of assets) {
            if (!models.has(modelName)) {
                await loadModel(modelName);
            }
        }
    }

    async function loadModel(name) {
        if (models.has(name)) return models.get(name);

        const fullPath = `${ASSET_BASE}/${name}`;
        try {
            const model = await loadGLTF(fullPath);
            if (model) {
                models.set(name, model);
                document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kaykit:modelLoaded', { detail: { name } }));
                return model;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn(`KayKitLoader: failed to load ${name}`, error);
        }
        return null;
    }

    window.KayKitLoader = {
        init,
        getConfig: () => config,
        getRoomConfig: (name) => config && config.rooms && config.rooms[name],
        hasModel: (name) => models.has(name),
        getModel: (name) => models.get(name),
        loadModel,
        loadRoomOnDemand,
        preloadRoomAssets
    };

    console.log('✅ KayKit Loader initialized');
})();

// Procedural Dungeon Generator for Schwarze Mühle - Keller
(function() {
    let currentLevel = 1;
    const SEED = 42;

    function seededRandom(seed) {
        let x = Math.sin(seed++) * 10000;
        return x - Math.floor(x);
    }

    function regenerateDungeon(roomName, level) {
        currentLevel = level || 1;
        const seed = SEED + currentLevel * 13;

        const config = {
            span: 48,
            wallHeight: 8,
            floor: { model: 'KayKit_DungeonRemastered_1.1_FREE/Assets/gltf/floor_tile_large.gltf.glb', span: 48 },
            walls: { model: 'KayKit_DungeonRemastered_1.1_FREE/Assets/gltf/wall.gltf.glb', span: 48, height: 8 },
            props: [],
            palette: {
                background: 0x0a0a0f,
                fog: 0x0a0a0f,
                ambient: 0.3,
                dir: 0xaa88ff,
                dirIntensity: 0.7,
                primary: 0xff3333,
                secondary: 0x8833ff,
                floor: 0x1a1a2e
            },
            spawn: [0, 0, 20]
        };

        // Generate props
        const propCount = Math.floor(10 + seededRandom(seed) * 20 * (level / 10));
        const grid = new Set();

        for (let i = 0; i < propCount; i++) {
            const x = Math.floor((seededRandom(seed + i * 7) - 0.5) * 40);
            const z = Math.floor((seededRandom(seed + i * 11) - 0.5) * 40);
            const key = `${x},${z}`;
            if (grid.has(key)) continue;
            grid.add(key);

            const propType = seededRandom(seed + i * 3);
            let model, scale = 1.5;

            if (propType < 0.4) {
                model = 'KayKit_DungeonRemastered_1.1_FREE/Assets/gltf/pillar.gltf.glb';
                scale = 2;
            } else if (propType < 0.7) {
                model = 'KayKit_DungeonRemastered_1.1_FREE/Assets/gltf/torch.gltf.glb';
                scale = 1.2;
            } else {
                model = 'KayKit_DungeonRemastered_1.1_FREE/Assets/gltf/wall_corner.gltf.glb';
                scale = 1.8;
            }

            config.props.push({
                model,
                position: [x, 0, z],
                scale,
                rotation: [0, seededRandom(seed + i * 5) * Math.PI * 2, 0]
            });
        }

        return config;
    }

    function setDungeonLevel(level) {
        currentLevel = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, level));
    }

    window.DungeonGenerator = {
        regenerateDungeon,
        setDungeonLevel,
        get currentLevel() { return currentLevel; }
    };

    console.log('✅ Dungeon Generator loaded');
})();

// Private Mode Indicator with SSE
(function() {
    let eventSource = null;
    const indicator = document.getElementById('privateIndicator');

    function connect() {
        if (eventSource) {
            eventSource.close();
        }

        eventSource = new EventSource('/api/status/stream');

        eventSource.onmessage = (event) => {
            try {
                const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
                updatePrivateMode(data.private_mode || false);
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('Private Mode: parse error', error);
            }
        };

        eventSource.onerror = () => {
            eventSource.close();
            setTimeout(connect, 3000);
        };
    }

    function updatePrivateMode(active) {
        if (indicator) {
            if (active) {
                indicator.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                indicator.classList.remove('active');
            }
        }
        if (window.Scene3D && typeof window.Scene3D.setPrivateMode === 'function') {
            window.Scene3D.setPrivateMode(active);
        }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', connect);
    } else {
        connect();
    }

    console.log('✅ Private Mode monitor started');
})();
